#### `std::string checkGeneratedAQLCollectionScope(const std::string &aql_query, const std::string &schema_context)`
- Source: `src/aql/llm_aql_handler.cpp`:395
- Brief: LLM-2 fix: extract collection names referenced in a generated AQL query via FOR IN <collection> patterns, and verify each against the caller-supplied schema_context.

  - `aql_query` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `schema_context` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: Return value.
- Details: aql_query Input parameter. schema_context Input parameter. Return value. Returns a non-empty error message when a collection outside the schema scope is found; returns "" when the scope check passes (or is skipped). Calls: empty(), spdlog::warn(), kForInPattern(), std::sregex_iterator(), begin(), end(), push_back(), str().

#### `std::vector< float > embed(const std::string &text) override`
- Source: `include/aql/llm_aql_embedding_bridge.h`:87
- Brief: Compute an embedding via the handler's executeEmbed() circuit.
- Parameters:
  - `text` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Return: Dense float embedding, or an empty vector on failure.
- Details: Embed. Delegates to LLMAQLHandler::executeEmbed(text). Any exception thrown by the handler (e.g. circuit-breaker open, LLM unavailable) is caught and an empty vector is returned, causing AQLFewShotExampleLibrary to fall back to Jaccard word-overlap ranking for that query. text Input text (may be empty; returns empty vector). Dense float embedding, or an empty vector on failure. text Input parameter. Return value. Calls: empty(), executeEmbed(), spdlog::warn(), what().
